#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

+// The send datatype selects each local body's position without packing it.  The
+// receive datatype is tightly packed Vec3 data, which is the layout used by
+// the force kernel.
+void exchangePositions(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies,
+                       std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyCounts,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyDisplacements,
+                       MPI_Datatype bodyPositionType,
+                       MPI_Datatype vec3Type) {
+    const void* sendBuffer = localBodies.empty() ? nullptr : localBodies.data();
+    void* receiveBuffer = allPositions.empty() ? nullptr : allPositions.data();
+
+    MPI_Allgatherv(sendBuffer,
+                   static_cast<int>(localBodies.size()),
+                   bodyPositionType,
+                   receiveBuffer,
+                   bodyCounts.data(),
+                   bodyDisplacements.data(),
+                   vec3Type,
+                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+// The send datatype selects each local body's position without packing it.  The
+// receive datatype is tightly packed Vec3 data, which is the layout used by
+// the force kernel.
+void exchangePositions(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies,
+                       std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyCounts,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyDisplacements,
+                       MPI_Datatype bodyPositionType,
+                       MPI_Datatype vec3Type) {
+    const void* sendBuffer = localBodies.empty() ? nullptr : localBodies.data();
+    void* receiveBuffer = allPositions.empty() ? nullptr : allPositions.data();
+
+    MPI_Allgatherv(sendBuffer,
+                   static_cast<int>(localBodies.size()),
+                   bodyPositionType,
+                   receiveBuffer,
+                   bodyCounts.data(),
+                   bodyDisplacements.data(),
+                   vec3Type,
+                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& localBodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions) {
+    const Vec3* const positions = allPositions.data();
+    const size_t numBodies = allPositions.size();
+
+    for (Body& body : localBodies) {
+        const double x = body.pos.x;
+        const double y = body.pos.y;
+        const double z = body.pos.z;
+        double Fx = 0.0;
+        double Fy = 0.0;
+        double Fz = 0.0;
+
+        // All ranks traverse the globally gathered positions in the original
+        // index order, preserving the per-body force calculation semantics.
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < numBodies; ++j) {
+            const double dx = positions[j].x - x;
+            const double dy = positions[j].y - y;
+            const double dz = positions[j].z - z;
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
+
             Fx += dx * invDist3;
             Fy += dy * invDist3;
             Fz += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+
+        body.vel.x += DT * Fx;
+        body.vel.y += DT * Fy;
+        body.vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
 }

The MPI implementation is now in place: contiguous body ranges are scattered once, positions are all-gathered each step without packing local data, and forces/integration execute only for the locally owned range. I’ll build it and compare one-rank versus multi-rank result serialization next.

+                          bodyCounts,
+                          bodyDisplacements,
+                          bodyPositionType,
+                          vec3Type);

+        int failure = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&localFailure, &failure, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+        if (failure == 0) {
+            const double localEnergy = computeLocalEnergy(localBodies, allPositions, localStart);
+            double finalEnergy = 0.0;
+            MPI_Reduce(&localEnergy, &finalEnergy, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
+                printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            }
         } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
+                if (failure == 1) {
+                    printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
+                } else if (failure == 2) {
+                    printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
+                } else {
+                    printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
+                }
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+            }
+            exitCode = 1;
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Type_free(&bodyPositionType);
+    MPI_Type_free(&vec3Type);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;

+void exchangePositions(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies,
+                       std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions,

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& localBodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions) {
+    const Vec3* const positions = allPositions.data();
+    const size_t numBodies = allPositions.size();

+        double Fy = 0.0;

+        // index order, preserving the per-body force calculation semantics.

+void exchangePositions(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies,
+                       std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyCounts,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyDisplacements,
+                       MPI_Datatype bodyPositionType,
+                       MPI_Datatype vec3Type) {
+    const void* sendBuffer = localBodies.empty() ? nullptr : localBodies.data();
+    void* receiveBuffer = allPositions.empty() ? nullptr : allPositions.data();
+
+    MPI_Allgatherv(sendBuffer,
+                   static_cast<int>(localBodies.size()),
+                   bodyPositionType,
+                   receiveBuffer,
+                   bodyCounts.data(),
+                   bodyDisplacements.data(),

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& localBodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions) {
+    const Vec3* const positions = allPositions.data();

+

+        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {

+                settings[1] = atoi(argv[++i]);

+                settings[4] = 1;

+// The send datatype selects each local body's position without packing it.  The
+// receive datatype is tightly packed Vec3 data, which is the layout used by
+// the force kernel.
+void exchangePositions(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies,
+                       std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyCounts,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyDisplacements,
+                       MPI_Datatype bodyPositionType,
+                       MPI_Datatype vec3Type) {
+    const void* sendBuffer = localBodies.empty() ? nullptr : localBodies.data();
+    void* receiveBuffer = allPositions.empty() ? nullptr : allPositions.data();
+
+    MPI_Allgatherv(sendBuffer,
+                   static_cast<int>(localBodies.size()),
+                   bodyPositionType,
+                   receiveBuffer,
+                   bodyCounts.data(),
+                   bodyDisplacements.data(),
+                   vec3Type,
+                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& localBodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions) {
+    const Vec3* const positions = allPositions.data();
+    const size_t numBodies = allPositions.size();
+
+    for (Body& body : localBodies) {
+        const double x = body.pos.x;
+        const double y = body.pos.y;
+        const double z = body.pos.z;
+        double Fx = 0.0;
+        double Fy = 0.0;
+        double Fz = 0.0;
+
+        // All ranks traverse the globally gathered positions in the original
+        // index order, preserving the per-body force calculation semantics.
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < numBodies; ++j) {
+            const double dx = positions[j].x - x;
+            const double dy = positions[j].y - y;
+            const double dz = positions[j].z - z;
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
+
             Fx += dx * invDist3;
             Fy += dy * invDist3;
             Fz += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+
+        body.vel.x += DT * Fx;
+        body.vel.y += DT * Fy;
+        body.vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
 }

+void exchangePositions(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies,
+                       std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyCounts,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyDisplacements,
+                       MPI_Datatype bodyPositionType,

+    void* receiveBuffer = allPositions.empty() ? nullptr : allPositions.data();
+

+                   bodyPositionType,
+                   receiveBuffer,
+                   bodyCounts.data(),
+                   bodyDisplacements.data(),

    14	constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
    15	constexpr double DT = 0.01;
    16	
    17	struct Vec3 {
    18	    double x, y, z;
    19	    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    20	};
    21	
    22	struct Body {
    23	    Vec3 pos;
    24	    Vec3 vel;
    25	};
    26	
    27	static_assert(std::is_standard_layout_v<Vec3> && sizeof(Vec3) == 3 * sizeof(double));
    28	static_assert(std::is_standard_layout_v<Body> && sizeof(Body) == 6 * sizeof(double));
    29	static_assert(offsetof(Body, pos) == 0 && offsetof(Body, vel) == sizeof(Vec3));
    30	
    31	void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    32	    for (auto& body : bodies) {
    33	        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    34	        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    35	        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    36	        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    37	        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    38	        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    39	    }
    40	}
    41	
    42	// The send datatype selects each local body's position without packing it.  The
    43	// receive datatype is tightly packed Vec3 data, which is the layout used by
    44	// the force kernel.
    45	void exchangePositions(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies,
    46	                       std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions,
    47	                       const std::vector<int>& bodyCounts,
    48	                       const std::vector<int>& bodyDisplacements,
    49	                       MPI_Datatype bodyPositionType,
    50	                       MPI_Datatype vec3Type) {
    51	    const void* sendBuffer = localBodies.empty() ? nullptr : localBodies.data();
    52	    void* receiveBuffer = allPositions.empty() ? nullptr : allPositions.data();
    53	
    54	    MPI_Allgatherv(sendBuffer,
    55	                   static_cast<int>(localBodies.size()),
    56	                   bodyPositionType,
    57	                   receiveBuffer,
    58	                   bodyCounts.data(),
    59	                   bodyDisplacements.data(),
    60	                   vec3Type,
    61	                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    62	}
    63	
    64	void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& localBodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions) {
    65	    const Vec3* const positions = allPositions.data();
    66	    const size_t numBodies = allPositions.size();
    67	
    68	    for (Body& body : localBodies) {
    69	        const double x = body.pos.x;
    70	        const double y = body.pos.y;
    71	        const double z = body.pos.z;
    72	        double Fx = 0.0;
    73	        double Fy = 0.0;
    74	        double Fz = 0.0;
    75	
    76	        // All ranks traverse the globally gathered positions in the original
    77	        // index order, preserving the per-body force calculation semantics.
    78	        for (size_t j = 0; j < numBodies; ++j) {
    79	            const double dx = positions[j].x - x;
    80	            const double dy = positions[j].y - y;
    81	            const double dz = positions[j].z - z;
    82	            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
    83	            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
    84	            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
    85	
    86	            Fx += dx * invDist3;
    87	            Fy += dy * invDist3;
    88	            Fz += dz * invDist3;
    89	        }
    90	
    91	        body.vel.x += DT * Fx;
    92	        body.vel.y += DT * Fy;
    93	        body.vel.z += DT * Fz;
    94	    }
    95	}
    96	
    97	void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& localBodies) {
    98	    for (Body& body : localBodies) {
    99	        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
   100	        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
   101	        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
   102	    }
   103	}
   104	
   105	double computeLocalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies,
   106	                          const std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions,
   107	                          int globalStart) {
   108	    double energy = 0.0;
   109	
   110	    for (const Body& body : localBodies) {
   111	        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +
   112	                         body.vel.y * body.vel.y +
   113	                         body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    // Each pair is owned by the rank that owns its lower global index.
   117	    for (size_t localIndex = 0; localIndex < localBodies.size(); ++localIndex) {
   118	        const Body& body = localBodies[localIndex];
   119	        const size_t globalIndex = static_cast<size_t>(globalStart) + localIndex;
   120	        for (size_t j = globalIndex + 1; j < allPositions.size(); ++j) {
   121	            const double dx = allPositions[j].x - body.pos.x;
   122	            const double dy = allPositions[j].y - body.pos.y;
   123	            const double dz = allPositions[j].z - body.pos.z;
   124	            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
   125	            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    return energy;
   130	}
   131	
   132	// Returns zero when valid, otherwise a code that identifies the failed check.
   133	int validationFailure(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies) {
   134	    for (const Body& body : localBodies) {
   135	        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
   136	            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
   137	            return 1;
   138	        }
   139	
   140	        constexpr double maxPosition = 1e6;
   141	        constexpr double maxVelocity = 1e6;
   142	        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPosition || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPosition ||
   143	            std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPosition) {
   144	            return 2;
   145	        }
   146	        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVelocity || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVelocity ||
   147	            std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVelocity) {
   148	            return 3;
   149	        }
   150	    }
   151	    return 0;
   152	}

   154	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   155	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   156	    printf("Options:\n");
   157	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
   158	    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
   159	    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
   160	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   161	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   162	}
   163	
   164	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   165	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   166	
   167	    int rank = 0;
   168	    int numRanks = 1;
   169	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   170	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);
   171	
   172	    // Settings are parsed once, then broadcast so every rank follows exactly
   173	    // the same collective-control path.
   174	    int settings[5] = {1024, 10, 0, 0, 0};
   175	    // settings: body count, step count, validate, print results, exit status.
   176	    if (rank == 0) {
   177	        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   178	            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   179	                settings[0] = atoi(argv[++i]);
   180	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   181	                settings[1] = atoi(argv[++i]);
   182	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   183	                settings[2] = 1;
   184	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   185	                settings[3] = 1;
   186	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   187	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   188	                settings[4] = 1;
   189	                break;
   190	            } else {
   191	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   192	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   193	                settings[4] = 2;
   194	                break;
   195	            }
   196	        }
   197	
   198	        if (settings[4] == 0 &&
   199	            (settings[0] < 0 || settings[1] < 0 || settings[0] > std::numeric_limits<int>::max() / 6)) {
   200	            printf("Number of bodies and steps must be non-negative; body count must fit MPI counts\n");
   201	            settings[4] = 2;
   202	        }
   203	    }
   204	
   205	    MPI_Bcast(settings, 5, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   206	    if (settings[4] != 0) {
   207	        MPI_Finalize();
   208	        return settings[4] == 1 ? 0 : 1;
   209	    }
   210	
   211	    const int numBodies = settings[0];
   212	    const int numSteps = settings[1];
   213	    const bool validate = settings[2] != 0;
   214	    const bool printResults = settings[3] != 0;
   215	
   216	    if (rank == 0) {
   217	        printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
   218	        printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
   219	        printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
   220	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   221	    }
   222	
   223	    std::vector<int> bodyCounts(numRanks);
   224	    std::vector<int> bodyDisplacements(numRanks);
   225	    const int bodiesPerRank = numBodies / numRanks;
   226	    const int extraBodies = numBodies % numRanks;
   227	    int bodyOffset = 0;
   228	    for (int process = 0; process < numRanks; ++process) {
   229	        bodyCounts[process] = bodiesPerRank + (process < extraBodies ? 1 : 0);
   230	        bodyDisplacements[process] = bodyOffset;
   231	        bodyOffset += bodyCounts[process];
   232	    }
   233	
   234	    std::vector<int> doubleCounts(numRanks);
   235	    std::vector<int> doubleDisplacements(numRanks);
   236	    for (int process = 0; process < numRanks; ++process) {
   237	        doubleCounts[process] = bodyCounts[process] * 6;
   238	        doubleDisplacements[process] = bodyDisplacements[process] * 6;
   239	    }
   240	
   241	    const int localBodyCount = bodyCounts[rank];
   242	    const int localStart = bodyDisplacements[rank];
   243	    std::vector<Body> localBodies(localBodyCount);
   244	
   245	    // Rank zero initializes with the exact serial random sequence, then hands
   246	    // each contiguous range to its owning rank.
   247	    {
   248	        std::vector<Body> initialBodies;
   249	        if (rank == 0) {
   250	            initialBodies.resize(numBodies);
   251	            randomizeBodies(initialBodies);
   252	        }
   253	        const void* initialSendBuffer = rank == 0 && !initialBodies.empty() ? initialBodies.data() : nullptr;
   254	        void* localReceiveBuffer = localBodies.empty() ? nullptr : localBodies.data();
   255	        MPI_Scatterv(initialSendBuffer,
   256	                     doubleCounts.data(),
   257	                     doubleDisplacements.data(),
   258	                     MPI_DOUBLE,
   259	                     localReceiveBuffer,
   260	                     localBodyCount * 6,
   261	                     MPI_DOUBLE,
   262	                     0,
   263	                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   264	    }
   265	
   266	    MPI_Datatype vec3Type;
   267	    MPI_Datatype bodyPositionType;
   268	    MPI_Type_contiguous(3, MPI_DOUBLE, &vec3Type);
   269	    MPI_Type_commit(&vec3Type);
   270	    MPI_Type_create_resized(vec3Type, 0, sizeof(Body), &bodyPositionType);
   271	    MPI_Type_commit(&bodyPositionType);
   272	
   273	    std::vector<Vec3> allPositions(numBodies);
   274	
   275	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   276	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   277	
   278	    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
   279	        exchangePositions(localBodies,
   280	                          allPositions,
   281	                          bodyCounts,
   282	                          bodyDisplacements,
   283	                          bodyPositionType,
   284	                          vec3Type);
   285	        computeForces(localBodies, allPositions);
   286	        integrateBodies(localBodies);
   287	    }
   288	
   289	    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   290	    double duration = 0.0;
   291	    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   292	
   293	    if (rank == 0) {
   294	        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", static_cast<long>(duration * 1000.0));
   295	    }
   296	
   297	    if (printResults) {
   298	        std::vector<double> bodyData;
   299	        if (rank == 0) {
   300	            bodyData.resize(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * 6);
   301	        }
   302	
   303	        void* resultReceiveBuffer = rank == 0 && !bodyData.empty() ? bodyData.data() : nullptr;
   304	        MPI_Gatherv(localBodies.empty() ? nullptr : localBodies.data(),
   305	                    localBodyCount * 6,
   306	                    MPI_DOUBLE,
   307	                    resultReceiveBuffer,
   308	                    doubleCounts.data(),
   309	                    doubleDisplacements.data(),
   310	                    MPI_DOUBLE,
   311	                    0,
   312	                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   313	
   314	        if (rank == 0) {
   315	            print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
   316	        }
   317	    }
   318	
   319	    int exitCode = 0;
   320	    if (validate) {
   321	        // The final integration changes positions, so synchronize them once
   322	        // more before assigning potential-energy pairs to their owner ranks.
   323	        exchangePositions(localBodies,
   324	                          allPositions,
   325	                          bodyCounts,
   326	                          bodyDisplacements,
   327	                          bodyPositionType,
   328	                          vec3Type);
   329	
   330	        const int localFailure = validationFailure(localBodies);
   331	        int failure = 0;
   332	        MPI_Allreduce(&localFailure, &failure, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   333	
   334	        if (failure == 0) {
   335	            const double localEnergy = computeLocalEnergy(localBodies, allPositions, localStart);
   336	            double finalEnergy = 0.0;
   337	            MPI_Reduce(&localEnergy, &finalEnergy, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   338	
   339	            if (rank == 0) {
   340	                printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
   341	                printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
   342	                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   343	            }
   344	        } else {
   345	            if (rank == 0) {
   346	                printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
   347	                if (failure == 1) {
   348	                    printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
   349	                } else if (failure == 2) {
   350	                    printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   351	                } else {
   352	                    printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   353	                }
   354	                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   355	            }
   356	            exitCode = 1;
   357	        }
   358	    }
   359	
   360	    MPI_Type_free(&bodyPositionType);
   361	    MPI_Type_free(&vec3Type);
   362	    MPI_Finalize();
   363	    return exitCode;
   364	}

codex
I’m tightening the communication path further: each rank stages its local positions directly in its segment of the receive buffer and uses in-place `MPI_Allgatherv`. That keeps the collective’s network buffer contiguous, which tends to scale better than repeatedly sending a strided derived datatype.
apply patch

+// Stage each rank's current positions in its own receive-buffer segment, then
+// use an in-place collective so the network payload is tightly packed Vec3s.
+void exchangePositions(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies,
+                       std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyCounts,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyDisplacements,
+                       int localStart,

+        double Fy = 0.0;
+        double Fz = 0.0;

+        // index order, preserving the per-body force calculation semantics.

+// Stage each rank's current positions in its own receive-buffer segment, then
+// use an in-place collective so the network payload is tightly packed Vec3s.
+void exchangePositions(const std::vector<Body>& localBodies,
+                       std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyCounts,
+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyDisplacements,
+                       int localStart,
+                       MPI_Datatype vec3Type) {
+    if (!localBodies.empty()) {
+        Vec3* const localPositions = allPositions.data() + localStart;
+        for (size_t body = 0; body < localBodies.size(); ++body) {
+            localPositions[body] = localBodies[body].pos;
+        }
+    }

+

+                   0,
+                   MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,
+                   receiveBuffer,
+                   bodyCounts.data(),
+                   bodyDisplacements.data(),

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& localBodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& allPositions) {
+    const Vec3* const positions = allPositions.data();
+    const size_t numBodies = allPositions.size();
+
+    for (Body& body : localBodies) {
+        const double x = body.pos.x;
+        const double y = body.pos.y;
+        const double z = body.pos.z;
+        double Fx = 0.0;
+        double Fy = 0.0;
+        double Fz = 0.0;
+
+        // All ranks traverse the globally gathered positions in the original
+        // index order, preserving the per-body force calculation semantics.
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < numBodies; ++j) {
+            const double dx = positions[j].x - x;
+            const double dy = positions[j].y - y;
+            const double dz = positions[j].z - z;
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
+

             Fy += dy * invDist3;
             Fz += dz * invDist3;

+        body.vel.y += DT * Fy;

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism in [nbody.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/nbody_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r2/nbody/nbody.cpp:45) and required MPI in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/nbody_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r2/nbody/CMakeLists.txt:9).

- Bodies are partitioned into contiguous rank-owned ranges.
- Rank 0 initializes the original deterministic sequence and scatters it.
- Each step uses in-place `MPI_Allgatherv` for current positions; each rank computes/integrates only its own bodies.
- Results, timing, and validation are reduced/gathered correctly with rank-0-only output.
- The executable remains `nbody`.

Verified with CMake Release builds and MPI runs, including 1 vs. 4 ranks with identical serialized results, zero bodies, and more ranks than bodies.

+                       const std::vector<int>& bodyCounts,

Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism in [nbody.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/nbody_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r2/nbody/nbody.cpp:45) and required MPI in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/nbody_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r2/nbody/CMakeLists.txt:9).

- Bodies are partitioned into contiguous rank-owned ranges.
- Rank 0 initializes the original deterministic sequence and scatters it.
- Each step uses in-place `MPI_Allgatherv` for current positions; each rank computes/integrates only its own bodies.
- Results, timing, and validation are reduced/gathered correctly with rank-0-only output.
- The executable remains `nbody`.

Verified with CMake Release builds and MPI runs, including 1 vs. 4 ranks with identical serialized results, zero bodies, and more ranks than bodies.
